## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter。比如你可以让我去看看某个代码仓库里挂掉的测试，修好了再告诉你。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal partner on this Mac. For example, I can get a failing test in one of your repos fixed and tell you once it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 도와드릴게요. 예를 들어 특정 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 고쳐 달라고 하시면 제가 작업을 열어 진행하고 결과를 알려드려요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 봐요. 좋은 밤 되세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届いたことを確かめながら、順番どおりに確実に届ける」通信方式で、UDPは「確認や順序保証をせず、速さを優先して投げっぱなしにする」通信方式です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales de una rama que nadie más usa, antes de integrarla. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está compartida o publicada, porque reescribir su historia rompería el trabajo de otros.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据按序、不丢不重地送到；UDP 是无连接、不保证可靠的传输，快但丢了就不管。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase to tidy your own local branch before sharing it, so history reads as a clean line. Merge when the branch is already shared or public, since rewriting it would break others' copies.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database on disk before the change is applied to the data itself. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart so committed changes survive and half-finished ones are rolled back.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我去把 compaction 那个 PR 里 review 提到的锁粒度改细，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户说：“接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点”

要做的：
- 找出这个 draft PR 的 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把锁改细（缩小临界区、减少全局锁的范围，按需要拆分锁）。
- 改动留在这个任务原来的分支上，提交到该分支。
- 跑相关测试，确认没有回归，并把测试结果附在报告里。
- 有并发相关的测试就一并跑，确认没有死锁或竞争。

不在范围内：
- 不合并到主分支，不推送，不改 PR 的状态（推送和合并都要等用户明确同意）。
- 其他 review 意见如果和锁粒度无关，先不动，只在报告里列出来。

完成标准：报告里写清楚改了哪些锁、为什么这样拆，并给出测试输出。如果有 review 意见我没法判断怎么改，直接在报告里说明，不要猜。
```

- 完成标准：给出优化前后的增量构建耗时对比，以及确认冷构建没有因此变坏。

```
(earlier) user: 每天早上帮我看一下周五北京到上海的高铁票价，600 以下的告诉我。
(earlier) partner ack: 好，每天早上去看一次。  → task "每天看周五京沪高铁票价" working; its report: 第 3 天：周五 G1 553 元（二等座余 12 张），G3 553 元（余 4 张），G7 626 元；600 以下的有 2 班。
(earlier) partner: 周五 600 以下的还有两班：G1 和 G3，都是 553 元，G3 只剩 4 张。
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "fares"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 做好了，已经填入第 3 天的数据：周五 600 元以下有 G1、G3 两班，各 553 元。
  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2164 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海 · 600 元以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">最近一次</h2>
  <div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="latest"></div></div>
  <h2 class="k-h">每天记录</h2>
  <div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="history"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
var LIMIT = 600;
function esc(s){ return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function(c){ return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]; }); }
function rowHtml(t){
  var cheap = t.price < LIMIT;
  return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">' + esc(t.no) +
    '</div><div class="k-row-sub">' + (t.seats != null ? '二等座余 ' + esc(t.seats) + ' 张' : '') +
    '</div><div class="k-row-value" style="color:' + (cheap ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)') + '">¥' + esc(t.price) + '</div></div>';
}
function render(days){
  days = days || [];
  var latest = days[days.length - 1];
  if (!latest) { document.getElementById('cnt').innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没记</span>'; return; }
  var cheap = latest.trains.filter(function(t){ return t.price < LIMIT; });
  document.getElementById('cnt').textContent = cheap.length + ' 班';
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = cheap.length ? cheap.map(function(t){ return t.no + ' ¥' + t.price; }).join('、') : '暂无 600 元以下的班次';
  document.getElementById('latest').innerHTML = '<div class="k-from">' + esc(latest.label) + '</div>' + latest.trains.map(rowHtml).join('');
  document.getElementById('history').innerHTML = days.slice().reverse().map(function(d){
    var c = d.trains.filter(function(t){ return t.price < LIMIT; }).length;
    return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">' + esc(d.label) + '</div><div class="k-row-sub">600 元以下 ' + c + ' 班</div></div>';
  }).join('') || '<div class="k-empty">还没有记录</div>';
  if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(cheap.length + ' 班 ≤600 元');
}
if (window.ducter) {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('days')).then(render);
  if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get('days')).then(render); });
}
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"days":[{"label":"第 3 天","trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}]}]}

Each round, write what stands now into it with app_data (app a010af2e0, set {key: value}; null removes a key); the app reads the keys days. Write whole values (a key's value is replaced), once a round. Then don't report_to_main for a round that only updated it: report only when something needs the perso
  [also used tools] app
```

### board-offer-go-en

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "bench"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the nightly benchmark now has a board, "hawdb nightly write benchmark", filled with nights 2 and 3. Each new night's report will land on it.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2062 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Each night's result lands here</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>No nights yet.</div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  function fmt(n) { return n == null ? "—" : (n / 1000).toFixed(0) + "k writes/s"; }
  function render(nights) {
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    var empty = document.getElementById("empty");
    list.textContent = "";
    nights = Array.isArray(nights) ? nights.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.night - a.night; }) : [];
    empty.hidden = nights.length > 0;
    if (nights.length) {
      var last = nights[0];
      document.getElementById("latest").textContent = "Night " + last.night + ": " + fmt(last.writes);
      document.getElementById("latest-sub").textContent =
        "p99 " + (last.p99 == null ? "—" : last.p99 + " ms") +
        " · compaction stalls " + (last.stalls == null ? "—" : last.stalls);
    }
    nights.forEach(function (n) {
      var row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "k-row";
      var main = document.createElement("div");
      main.className = "k-row-main";
      main.textContent = "Night " + n.night;
      var sub = document.createElement("div");
      sub.className = "k-row-sub";
      sub.textContent = "p99 " + (n.p99 == null ? "—" : n.p99 + " ms") +
        " · stalls " + (n.stalls == null ? "—" : n.stalls);
      main.appendChild(sub);
      var val = document.createElement("div");
      val.className = "k-row-value";
      val.textContent = fmt(n.writes);
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("nights")).then(render);
  ducter.onChange(function () { Promise.resolve(ducter.get("nights")).then(render); });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":2,"writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":null},{"night":3,"writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

### app-make-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 给我做个小应用，记一下这趟北京出差的花销，按天列，带个合计
  [partner message] 做好了，记一笔就自动加总。
  [an app card under the message: made "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 3805 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差 · 合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天的花销会按日期列在这里</div>
  </div>

  <div id="days"></div>

  <details class="k-add" open>
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="d" placeholder="11-25"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>花在哪</label><input class="k-input" id="w" placeholder="午饭、打车、酒店…"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="a" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
  </details>
</main>
<style>
  .k-card { margin: 12px 0; }
  .k-h { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: baseline; font-weight: 600; color: var(--d-ink); padding: 6px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--d-line); }
  .k-row { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; gap: 12px; padding: 8px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--d-line); color: var(--d-ink); }
  .k-row-main { flex: 1; }
  .k-row-value { font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
  .k-add { margin-top: 16px; }
  .k-add > sum …   }).join('');
    if (items.length) {
      $('total').innerHTML = fmt(total);
      $('sub').textContent = days.length + ' 天，' + items.length + ' 笔';
      ducter.summary(items.length + ' 笔，合计 ' + fmt(total));
    } else {
      $('total').innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
      $('sub').textContent = '每天的花销会按日期列在这里';
      $('days').innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">还没记，下面记第一笔</div>';
      ducter.summary('还没记');
    }
  }

  $('save').onclick = () => {
    const a = parseFloat($('a').value);
    const w = $('w').value.trim();
    if (!a || !w) return;
    items.push({ d: $('d').value.trim() || today(), w, a });
    $('w').value = '';
    $('a').value = '';
    save();
  };

  // tap a row once to arm, again to remove
  $('days').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const r = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!r) return;
    const i = +r.dataset.i;
    if (r.dataset.armed) { items.splice(i, 1); save(); }
    else {
      r.dataset.armed = '1';
      r.querySelector('.k-row-main').textContent = '再点一下删除';
      setTimeout(render, 2500);
    }
  });

  ducter.onChange(() => { items = ducter.get(KEY) || []; render(); });
  $('d').value = today();
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

  document.getElementById("empty").hidden=xs.length>0;
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=xs.length?fmt(total):"¥0";

}

<script>
var feeds = [];
function p(n){ return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }
function today(){
  var d = new Date();
  return d.getFullYear() + '-' + p(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + p(d.getDate());
}
function nowHM(){
  var d = new Date();
  return p(d.getHours()) + ':' + p(d.getMinutes());
}
function render(){
  var day = today();
  var list = feeds.filter(function(f){ return f.t.slice(0, 10) === day; })
                  .sort(function(a, b){ return a.t < b.t ? -1 : 1; });
  var total = list.red … Name = 'k-sum-value';
    tv.textContent = total + ' ml';
    sub.textContent = list.length + ' 次,最近 ' + list[list.length - 1].t.slice(11);
    ducter.summary(list.length + ' 次,合计 ' + total + 'ml');
  }
  var ul = document.getElementById('list');
  if (!list.length) {
    ul.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">今天还没有记录</div>';
  } else {
    ul.innerHTML = list.map(function(f){
      return '<div class="k-row"><span class="k-row-main">' + f.t.slice(11) +
             '</span><span class="k-row-value">' + f.ml + ' ml</span></div>';
    }).join('');
  }
}
function load(){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('feeds')).then(function(v){
    feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  });
}
document.getElementById('add').onclick = function(){
  var tEl = document.getElementById('time');
  var mEl = document.getElementById('ml');
  var t = tEl.value.trim() || nowHM();
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t)) return;
  var hm = t.split(':');
  var ml = parseFloat(mEl.value);
  if (!ml) return;
  feeds.push({ t: today() + ' ' + p(+hm[0]) + ':' + hm[1], ml: ml });
  ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
  tEl.value = '';
  mEl.value = '';
  render();
};
ducter.onChange(function(){ load(); });
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"t":"2026-11-25 06:10","ml":120},{"t":"2026-11-25 09:05","ml":90}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Made it: the four PRs on one page. Tap one when it lands and it crosses off, and the count at the top drops.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1620 characters): <main class="k-app">
<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">of 4 · tap one when it lands</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
<div class="k-card"><ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul></div>
<style>
.landed .k-row-main,.landed .k-row-sub{text-decoration:line-through;opacity:.5}
.k-row{cursor:pointer;list-style:none}
</style>
<script>
var PRS=ducter.get("prs") || [];
var landed={};
function el(tag,cls,text){var e=document.createElement(tag);if(cls)e.className=cls;if(text!=null)e.textContent=text;return e;}
function render(){
  var list=document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML='';
  var left=0;
  PRS.forEach(function(p){
    var done=!!landed[p.id];
    if(!done) left++;
    var li=el('li',done?'k-row landed':'k-row');
    li.onclick=function(){toggle(p.id);};
    var main=el('div','k-row-main',(done?'✓ ':'')+p.repo);
    li.appendChild(main);
    li.appendChild(el('div','k-row-sub',p.what));
    li.appendChild(el('div','k-row-sub',p.wait));
    list.appendChild(li);
  });
  document.getElementById('left').textContent=String(left);
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent=left===0?'all landed':'of '+PRS.length+' · tap one when it lands';
  if(window.ducter&&ducter.summary) ducter.summary(left+' waiting');
}
function toggle(id){
  landed[id]=!landed[id];
  ducter.set('landed',landed);
  render();
}
function load(){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function(v){
    landed=v||{};
    render();
  });
}
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#412","what":"Compaction cut","wait":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday"},{"id":"10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","what":"Retry on 502","wait":"CI is failing"},{"id":"1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","what":"Flaky compaction test","wait":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#418","what":"Apps bar","wait":"Still a draft"}]}

<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" value="北京出差 11/20–21"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" value="2026-11-20"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>分类</label>
<div class="k-chips" id="cats">
<button type="button" class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button>
<button type="button" class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button>
</div></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</div>
</details>
</main>
<script>
var cat="交通";
function esc(s){return String(s==null?"":s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]})}
function money(n){return "¥"+Number(n||0).t … al) : '<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
  document.getElementById("sub").textContent = xs.length ? xs.length+" 笔" : "添了就会算合计";
  document.getElementById("empty").style.display = xs.length ? "none" : "";
  document.getElementById("list").innerHTML = xs.map(function(x){
    return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">'+esc(x.trip)+'<div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.cat||"")+' '+esc(x.day||"")+'</div></div><div class="k-row-value">'+money(x.amt)+'</div></div>';
  }).join("");
  ducter.summary(xs.length ? xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+money(total) : "还没记");
}
document.querySelectorAll("#cats button").forEach(function(b){
  b.onclick=function(){
    cat=b.dataset.c;
    document.querySelectorAll("#cats button").forEach(function(o){o.setAttribute("aria-pressed", o===b ? "true" : "false")});
  };
});
document.getElementById("save").onclick=function(){
  var amt=parseFloat(document.getElementById("amt").value);
  if(!amt) return;
  var xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:document.getElementById("trip").value,day:document.getElementById("day").value,cat:cat,amt:amt});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  document.getElementById("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥到北京南"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}
  [also used tools] app

(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让修 TTL 测试的那个任务再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻，补好告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

在现有的假时钟 TTL 测试基础上，再补一条测试：把时钟推进到key正好到期的那一刻（恰好等于 TTL），断言此时该值已经过期（取不到）；同时在到期前一刻（比 TTL 少 1 毫秒或一个最小单位）断言值仍然存在。

完成标准：新测试通过，并且连续跑 20 次都通过；把跑测试的输出报告出来。
范围外：不要改动其他测试或生产代码的过期逻辑，除非新测试暴露了边界 bug（那种情况先报告给我，不要直接改）。不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR。提交到你现在的分支即可。
```
